SCOPE AND CLASSIFICATION
------------------------
Products: DongClock Server Time Basic 1.0.0 and Pro 1.0.0
Target: x86_64-pc-windows-msvc
Rust normal-edge packages resolved for each product: 293
Rust build-dependencies and Cargo dev/test dependencies are excluded. A proc-macro reached through a normal edge is listed separately as a build-time tool and is not described as a runtime library.
Frontend runtime package: zod 3.25.76. TypeScript, Vite, tsx, and the Tauri CLI are build/development-only and are not included in the frontend runtime inventory.
Microsoft Edge WebView2 is an external platform runtime installed or bootstrapped separately; it is not bundled as a Rust or frontend library in the DongClock application binary.
No package in this locked production graph declares GPL, LGPL, or AGPL license metadata.

APPENDIX: How to apply the Apache License to your work.

   boilerplate notice, with the fields enclosed by brackets "[]"

   the brackets!)  The text should be enclosed in the appropriate
   comment syntax for the file format. We also recommend that a
